However, we have sometimes developed a consensual practice in such a way that, if two applications are made on the same or a very related subject, we proceed in such a way that each application is justified by the applicant in accordance with the provisions of § 33 and then a joint debate is held if the applicants agree. I believe that if some figures relating to the flood disaster and the subsequent repair phase are made comprehensible to the Austrians, then the decision will be taken on 24 March. ==References====External links== We have spent €1,9 billion in Austria for about 186,000 of this disaster on the part of the Federal Government and the countries in the current state. Last weekend, the balance sheet of Rot-Grün was seen in the German Bundestag, where 1 million euros were spent on federal funds for 5 million people affected. I therefore believe that in the case of flood victims and repair services in this area, we must not be under the umbrella of the Austrian Federal Government, but also of the countries concerned. At the time of the government meeting last week, we had already paid out more money to those concerned than the Federal Government paid the flood victims in 1991 and the Federal Government climate in 1995. The figures are comprehensible for all Austrians who are politically interested in the respective budgets. I therefore believe that immediate help is the most important thing. However, I can also thank the Federal Minister for the fact that in the course of this disaster thousands of Austrians in a truly convincing way were volunteering not only in the well-known good Austrian bluelight organizations – fire brigade, red cross and other auxiliary services – but also that the young men and the sub-offenders and officers of the Austrian Federal Army have also been employed and thousands of Austrians have also done voluntary work outside the auxiliary services. I believe that this willingness of the Austrians in Europe is exemplary. [[Congratulations with the liberals and representatives of the ÖVP.]] However, as well as the Federal Minister responsible for Veterinary Affairs, I should like to express my warmest thanks to the fact that my suggestions were implemented so quickly by all veterinary directives in all the federal states and by many volunteers on the ground that two days after the drop in the water level in all the countries concerned, the disease commissions could be dissolved and more than 290,000 animals destroyed with the help of 30 Federal Helicopters and very many volunteers who helped in the field could be properly disposed of. In this way, one of the dangers to Austrian agriculture associated with this flood situation could be minimized. [[Abg. Dr. Khol: Very important!]] Thank you for that! [[Congratulations with the liberals and representatives of the ÖVP.]] Ladies and gentlemen! In addition, we have moved a lot in the last three years but also in the social field: The last pension reform and the last pension adjustment were already the subject of the debate several times today. I can therefore only comment on the following supplementary comments: We have not only been able to increase the retirement age, but we have also been able to improve access to invalidity pensions for a group that was particularly dear to me as a social minister, namely for unskilled workers without professional protection. Friend.]] Ladies and gentlemen of the opposition! In your speeches, you have also addressed the ambulance charges. I ask you: How are you going to take the 760 million schillings that this measure has brought to the present state of affairs? This means that in order to find in health insurance the same means as today, you have to tax each insured person 300 s annually, including those who are exempt from the ambulance fees today. This means that all those whose income is below the compensation rate, all those exempt from the prescription fees, all families with children, all pregnant women, all those suffering from chronic diseases, and also all those who are exempt from the ambulance fees on the basis of an acute need for treatment. I do not think, ladies and gentlemen, of the opposition that, when they calculate, the Austrians will find your model to safeguard the health site of Austria more attractive than the model of this Federal Government. More about unemployment: 199 684 unemployed at the end of August this year are clearly too much. But one should also tell the Austrians what the development of these unemployment figures looked like. In August 1999, 180.000 Austrians were unemployed throughout Austria, in Vienna 62,592. – In August 2000, there were 155 000 in Austria, not very 56 000 in Vienna. – Then came the Vienna election, and with it came the turnaround: In Austria about 171,000 unemployed and in Vienna around 62 000 – those were the figures from August 2001. In August 2002: 199 684 unemployed in Austria, and 70 345 unemployed in Vienna. Since the Vienna election, there has been a turnaround in Vienna. In the past, the unemployment rate in Vienna was 1 : 3. But now the increase in unemployment in Vienna is 66 percent of the total growth rate in Austria! But in Vienna, ladies and gentlemen of Social Democracy, you have been solely responsible since the election! [[Congratulations with the liberals and representatives of the ÖVP.]] You have created a strengthening effect on unemployment with your policy in Vienna, and this must also be said clearly to the Austrians before a choice before they make their decision. [[Intrusion of the Abg. Silhavy.]] You have criticised – you too, Mr Van der Bellen – that the health system in Austria will not be properly continued by this Federal Government. Mr Van der Bellen, I should like to point out that the training of nurses in the field of health care in the Federal Republic of Germany is much better and there is also a training of nurses who go far beyond the country's offer, but in the urban area, unfortunately, the willingness to opt for a care profession is in decline. For this reason, Mr Van der Bellen, for example, we have reached a social agreement with Slovakia, because it is socially not fair to give a weaker country like Slovakia the training costs for the health and care professions, in order to oblige the trainees in the richer Austria and in Vienna. This is a social transfer which we do not consider justified, Mr Van der Bellen. We want a social transfer, which ensures that in Austria we find those who are in the care professions needed for us again among the people of our own country. We have therefore conducted negotiations with the federal states, which, according to the agreement of all the federal states, are to be concluded in December of this year and in which it is a matter of finally creating a common curriculum for the care professions, for the professions of the elderly, for the family and for the disabled, and thus finally implementing the arrow study in this area. We have created new professions in our field at a high quality for three important groups. I can remind you: After decades of conflict, we have finally been able to implement the rules on rescue ants. We have been able to significantly improve the law of midwives. We have also launched something with the Heilmassurgesetz, which will create a new possibility of medical care in the future especially in the integrated social slogans for people with disabilities or after a hospital stay. I would like to stress at this point that I have never been able to make a multi-class health policy in Austria, but I have insisted that the multi-class health policy that I found on my accession – namely good quality in the urban area and a clear deficit in the rural area – will finally be corrected! [[Congratulations with the liberals and representatives of the ÖVP.]] I should also like to point out that one third of all vacancies for established practitioners and established specialists in the two supposedly so economical federal states are not occupied by Upper Austria and Vorarlberg. I also say quite clearly: Although this non-retirement of positions in these two federal states has led to a more economical economy of social security companies, it has certainly not led to access to medical services in the rural area being as good as in the urban sector. That is why I call for clear support. [[Congratulations with the liberals and representatives of the ÖVP.]] In this area, when we look at social expenditure as a whole, we can see the following: We started with 26.7 percent of the gross domestic product in 1990, we were 28.5 percent relative to the gross domestic product in 1998, and we also had a continuous increase of 30.4 percent in 2001. We have not done any social reduction on the part of this Federal Government, but we have made an extension of the social state – as this federal government has fixed in its government agreement, and that is good! [[Congratulations with the liberals and the ÖVP.]] Ladies and gentlemen! As milestones of my house – when you look at the 1999 election platform – child care money, treatment and hospice were laid down. For me, it is also particularly gratifying that today's package reintroduces the free movement of apprentices abolished by the Social Democrats – an important contribution especially for families who have a bad income so that they can afford to give apprentices in training again. [[Congratulations with the liberals and the ÖVP.]] I am also happy that, for two years, it will be possible for disabled people to make a clear contribution to medical practices, public buildings and businesses. Ladies and gentlemen, I think it is overdue to give disabled people the same opportunity, even in Austria, to reach hotels, medical practices, pharmacies and other public institutions, as in other comparable countries, such as America or Italy, in which this is more advanced than here. ParlaMint-AT_2002-09-19-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00115_d7e15411	Mr President. Mr. State Secretary! Estimated Mayor's College! Your claim for 100 percent compensation for flood victims in honour, however, I remind you that in the last decades of socialist government only VAT has been refunded, i.e. 20 percent. In return, this regulation looks quite different. As a result, I will be happy to explain them to you in detail. Dear ladies and gentlemen! In August of this year, there was a flood event, as it has not been remembered since human thought. At the Danube one speaks of a century high, at the camp of a millennium event. In a very short time, precipitation occurred, accounting for 30 to 50 percent of the total annual rainfall, within hours. Even in my home community, which is located at the European headwater divorce, disaster prevention had to be given, because a stream, which is usually a romantic, a few meters wide stream, has developed into a tearing stream. As a result, entire valley floors were flooded. Everything that got in the way of these masses of water was simply washed away. Within a very short time it became known that fish ponds break, streets are washed away, bridges are collapsed. But just as the masses of water are swollen, the will of the population to face this challenge has also increased, "anged". What I have experienced in the course of this disaster day as mayor and leader is insurmountable in terms of solidarity and willingness to help. People who in the past sent letters only via the lawyer have filled shoulder to shoulder sandbags. Young people who mentioned to me that they had "no bock" to society have dragged these heavy sandbags to the right places for hours. Firemen and volunteer helpers were busy around the clock with reeds, mountains and helpers. Some of them have only given themselves three, four hours of sleep in 48 hours, simply and simply overhuman. This fact is also due to the fact that many things were prevented at the last moment and thus even greater damage was removed. After the masses of water were slowly diverted, the damage came to light. The community's claims collection committees – and I would remind you, Mr. Col. – have assessed them in a fair form as a basis for compensation. In Lower Austria alone, more than 9 000 claims have been received from private individuals and companies. Tragic individual fates have been known: old people whose homes have been washed away and who can no longer exert the power to rebuild; young families whose homes are no longer habitable but who still bear the old debts; or the case of a young man who is tied to the wheelchair after a motorcycle accident in the previous year and whose general-renovated house was devastated by the camp floods. In view of such circumstances, every reasonable thinking person is fully aware and aware that flood aid is top priority. My Dear Ones Ladies and gentlemen! Crisis management to cope with the natural disaster has worked perfectly. It was not only the forces of fire brigade, the German army and the Red Cross and many other aid organizations who were quickest on the ground, but also the financial support. Within a short period of time, over 9 000 cases of damage in Lower Austria have been handled and already in contact with over 8 200. [[Abg. May. Gaßner: How much percent did they get?]] The rate of funding has already made up as much as 100 percent in the past of your government. That's the serious difference. You know that in individual cases there are funding rates that go far beyond 50 percent, and you know exactly that it is due to our media and especially to the population, which unites into a solidarity community and has created a fundraising action, as Austria has never seen before – I claim how the world has never seen it. [[Congratulations on the ÖVP.]] [[Abg. May. Gäßner: Government solidarity is needed!]] The solidarity of the population with the injured is to the most extent given, Mr President. [[Abg. May. Gaßner: The population yes, the government no!]] I say a warm thank you to all those responsible at the federal level; above all to our Federal Chancellor, including his enormous commitment at EU level, in relation to the European disaster fund. I would like to thank our Minister Willi Molterer and the whole country. But my special thanks go to my fire brigade comrades who worked until the fall, to all the volunteer helpers, the professionals of the bra and the Red Cross. In the future, we will have to recognize: That was a sign of nature. We will have to accept that more space will have to be given to water in the course of the spatial planning programmes. We must not go back to energy policy in relation to alternative energies. That's where you can rely on me. But one thing has to be clear to all of us: Such an event cannot be prevented by rules and standards. The high water at the camp, a calculated flood at 200 cubic meters per second, has actually been 800 cubic meters per second in peak times. It's not calculable, it's not manageable. We must also be aware of this. – Thank you for your attention.

While investments have been made in many European countries, Austria has declined. This means that Austria, if we had moved in the OECD section, would now have to invest more for education than it actually invests by 1 billion schilling. – This balance sheet is not a fame sheet, quite the opposite: This is the balance sheet of three years of education reduction and in no way what you call here as a great educational offensive! It reads: Investment in education is to be invested! – I’ll take you Your procedure in this area is also a bit bad. ParlaMint-AT_2002-09-19-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00115_d7e16338	Mr President. Mr. Federal Minister! Mr State Secretary! High house! Ladies and gentlemen! I think the government balance over the last two and a half years can really be seen. By the end of September 2002, the current legislative period has succeeded in meeting key points of the government programme, in particular implementing a number of important projects. These successes could be achieved in an extremely difficult economic environment – as the economy has significantly weakened in all leading industrial nations. Despite the internationally difficult economic situation, Austria has been able to claim and partly has much better economic data than our largest trading partner Germany. In addition, the flood disaster throughout Austria presents a very hard test and requires urgent action to be taken by the government coalition by means of the flood-offering and reconstruction law. From the fullness of parliamentary initiatives, the most important examples of a success report are the "rehandling" of new work, which is carried out even at the time of self-promulgation; the equality of workers and employees finally reached after years. In Austria's anti-atomic policy, too, decisive steps have been taken, such as the consistent continuation of the Austrian anti-atomic policy at European level, a three-party proposal concerning the further process at European level, which the Greens have also agreed to in addition to the Austrian People's Party and the Liberals. In the plant law, which is particularly important for the Austrian economy, there were clear accelerations and debureaucratisation of approval procedures in accordance with the so-called one-stop shop principle. This, of course, promotes competitiveness and brings huge savings. We have also achieved greater success in other areas, especially in the field of worker protection. Just think about the various procedural simplifications. Foreign employment has also been significantly reduced for the first time in 2002. In the procurement sector, a federal procurement organisation was established as part of the debureaucratisation of the federal government. In the field of education, an expansion of school autonomy was achieved on the basis of behavioural agreements and school partnership. The transfer from the Polytechnic School to the AHS is a particularly important part of the new education policy for us, as it will create greater opportunities. It has also led to an appreciation of the primary schools, which are, for a long time, more "situated on the ground". Through the Federal Minister's Head, a very decisive security for Austrian consumers was also created in connection with the fight against BSE. There were also permanent arrangements for funding required by the BSE crisis. It was also important to extend the ban on feeding animal meal. In the previous year, the achievement of the zero deficit was particularly successful. Thus, after the Federal Government had taken over a deficit of 2,200 billion schilling in the first place, no further debt was made in 2001, which, of course, has led to a greater margin for ensuring full employment and increasing prosperity – concerns that will continue to be pursued. In the field of safety, we should point out, in particular, that it has succeeded in introducing the mandatory blood test in the event of suspected drug intake. We have recently started specifically the new youth programme, which is very important. Youth in particular is to be given greater opportunities for the future. There have been significant increases in family allowances. In the field of financial market supervision, it was very necessary to finally establish a consensus and clarity as regards the conduct of the control bodies. The trade system has also been dedusted, and obstacles have been removed, making it easier to access independent entrepreneurship. The flood compensation has been referred to several times today, and I think it is a particularly important contribution to solidarity. I am proud of this petition, because in 1996, it was the Greens – and only these – who brought this Biomedicine Convention and its contents to the media and to the public, and they were suddenly able to raise them to a level on which we had to discuss it in Austria. Whoever deals with the Biomedicine Convention knows that there are passages in it that present a threat to many people in Austria. For example, there is the possibility that inadmissible persons can be used for medical purposes without their own benefit and even if it is a risk to the person concerned. That is, mentally disabled people, old, dementia-sick people as well as people in the coma would have to keep "supply rabbits" of medicine under this Convention. That can't happen. Although there are still laws in Austria that ensure a higher standard, if Austria had ratified the Council of Europe's Biomedicine Convention, these Austrian standards would have slipped very quickly down in the interests of science. When it comes to medicine and science, there is always a lot of money behind it. Therefore, the disabled people in Austria wish that, before Austria ratifys the Biomedicine Convention, there should be a constitutional provision ensuring that no attempts can be made on unfit persons. Only when these standards are guaranteed can we discuss ratification. We disabled people have managed to bring this issue to Parliament. There was a hearing in the petition committee. Just as three, four years ago – up to the last year – it was thought to bring it quickly to Parliament, to ratify it and to get away with it, it did not succeed. There the people concerned put themselves on the wheels and on their feet and fought for their rights. I'm so proud of that! [[Congratulations with the Greens and representatives of the SPÖ.]] 66 000 signatures have been collected, as individuals! I think this success can be seen. That's why: Everyone should take the opportunity to fight for their rights. Whoever doesn't fight, he accepts. But you should not accept it if you are of a different opinion or if you belong to one of those groups that are not yet equal in our society. Equality is the most important thing. Every person here in this Parliament should have the opportunity to express his opinion. We MEPs have the duty to represent the interests of citizens. The Committee on Petitions must also comply with this task in the future. We will not vote in favour of the collective report for the following reason: We are not aware of the way in which the work in the Committee on Petitions has been done, namely that two thirds of citizens' initiatives and petitions have simply been shubbled. We reject the report because we are not satisfied with the content of these citizens' initiatives.

Applications shall have the following wording: [[Quoted print version removed]] Request of Deputies Mag. Werner Kogler, Dr. Peter Pilz, colleagues on the establishment of a committee of inquiry in accordance with § 33 GOG on the activities related to the so-called catch-hunting post-purchasing The National Council wants to decide: An investigation committee shall be set up to examine: 1. Examining the legality of all procedures and decisions within the procurement process concerning the so-called catch-hunting post-purchasing 2. The Committee of Inquiry shall, by means of surveys of oral and written information on the subject-matter of the investigation and by means of an understanding of the files of the listed Federal Ministries, respectively. Parties, organisations, companies, in connection with the subject-matter of the investigation, shall review all matters concerning legal and political responsibilities. In particular, the following responsibilities shall be included in the review: Investment and responsibility of Federal Chancellor Dr. Schüssel, Vice-Cancellor Riess-Passer and the Federal Ministers for Finance (Grasser), Economics (Bartenstein) and National Defense (Scheibner), their cabinet and the Ministries led by them in the course of the entire procurement process for the purchase of combat aircraft; the participation of national leaders in the entire procurement process, in particular in the context of the so-called compensation operations; Investment of the political parties in Austria; Investment of close-to-party organisations and preparatory organisations; Investment of the Chamber of Commerce and Industry; Investment of party-related companies, in particular the PR agency for the Eurofighter lobbying "100% Comunications" and its management; Investing companies that benefit from the alleged compensation transactions. The undersigned Members request that an investigation committee be set up in relation to: 5 SPÖ, 4 FPÖ, 4 ÖVP, 1 Green. Reasons: ==References== In July 2002, the Federal Government announced a type decision on the purchase of interception hunters. Around the decision to enter into contractual negotiations with the EEAS consortium, numerous inconsistencies have become known in connection with the procurement process. Among other things, in the course of the conflicts within the government party FPÖ and the Federal Government itself, it has been hard to suspect that essential processes and individual decisions are not carried out in accordance with the legal and procurement rules. Several members of the Federal Government were publicly and mediaically charged with serious suspicions. "Since in the last few days it has become clear to me that the economic interests with the takeover of the FPÖ in its political ability to act..." said Landeshuptmann Dr. Jörg Haider (14. 9. 2002, Kärntner FPÖ Pressedienst) For example, Ms. Dr. Susanne Riess-Passible representative of her party held a written statement stating that no one should always benefit from the purchase of new jets in any way (profile 30/02; Page 18) Or the former FPÖ Secretary General and current EADS Lobbyist Gernot Rumpold: "That's like Uganda. If you don't show up in Austria with the suitcase, nothing will work" (Format 38/02; Page 46) In formal terms, the Members who have been signed call for a debate on this request (GoG § 33(2)).
